────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2. Global Coordinator
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Routes cognition across the cluster.

# distributed/coordinator.py

class GlobalCoordinator:

    def __init__(self):

        self.nodes = {}

    def register_node(
        self,
        node_id,
        node
    ):

        self.nodes[node_id] = node

    def select_node(
        self,
        task_type
    ):

        for node in self.nodes.values():

            if node.specialization == task_type:
                return node

        return {"status": "unavailable"}

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  5. Inter-Agent Communication Bus
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Agents communicate globally.

# swarm/communication.py

class CommunicationBus:

    def __init__(self):
        self.messages = []

    def send(
        self,
        sender,
        receiver,
        message
    ):

        self.messages.append({
            "sender": sender,
            "receiver": receiver,
            "message": message
        })

    def inbox(self, receiver):

        return [
            m for m in self.messages
            if m["receiver"] == receiver
        ]

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1. Self-Observation Engine
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

The system monitors itself continuously.

# evolution/self_observer.py

class SelfObserver:

    def analyze_system(
        self,
        metrics
    ):

        problems = []

        if metrics["latency"] > 2000:
            problems.append(
                "reasoning_too_slow"
            )

        if metrics["memory_failures"] > 5:
            problems.append(
                "memory_instability"
            )

        return problems

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  4. Agent Mutation System
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Agents evolve over time.

# evolution/agent_mutation.py

class AgentMutation:

    def mutate(
        self,
        agent
    ):

        upgraded = dict(agent)

        upgraded["reasoning_depth"] = (
            upgraded.get(
                "reasoning_depth",
                1
            ) + 1
        )

        return upgraded

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2. Stability Engine
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Monitors cognitive coherence.

# governance/stability.py

class StabilityEngine:

    def evaluate(
        self,
        metrics
    ):

        unstable = False

        if metrics["contradictions"] > 10:
            unstable = True

        if metrics["hallucination_rate"] > 0.3:
            unstable = True

        return {
            "stable": not unstable
        }

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3. Contradiction Detection
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Detects incompatible beliefs/goals.

# governance/contradictions.py

class ContradictionDetector:

    def detect(self, beliefs):

        conflicts = []

        for a in beliefs:
            for b in beliefs:

                if a == f"not_{b}":
                    conflicts.append((a, b))

        return conflicts

# governance/rollback.py

class RollbackSystem:

    def rollback(
        self,
        version
    ):

        return f"restored_{version}"

Civilizations vote on global actions.

# civilization/voting.py

class CivilizationVoting:

    def decide(self, votes):

        approvals = votes.count(True)

        return approvals > (
            len(votes) / 2
        )

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Step 3 — Event Bus
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Agents communicate asynchronously.

# core/event_bus.py

import asyncio

class EventBus:

    def __init__(self):

        self.queue = asyncio.Queue()

    async def publish(
        self,
        event
    ):

        await self.queue.put(event)

    async def consume(self):

        return await self.queue.get()

Example Tool Router
# tools/router.py

class ToolRouter:

    def execute(
        self,
        tool,
        payload
    ):

        if tool == "bash":
            return self.run_bash(payload)

        if tool == "python":
            return self.run_python(payload)

    def run_bash(self, command):

        import subprocess

        result = subprocess.run(
            command,
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True
        )

        return result.stdout

# api/ws.py

from fastapi import WebSocket

class SocketManager:

    async def connect(
        self,
        websocket: WebSocket
    ):

        await websocket.accept()

    async def send(
        self,
        websocket,
        data
    ):

        await websocket.send_json(data)

Next Stage: Production Intelligence Hardening Layer

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1. Hard State Management (Critical Upgrade)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Right now systems usually break because state is messy.

You need a single source of truth.

# core/state.py

class GlobalState:

    def __init__(self):
        self.tasks = {}
        self.agents = {}
        self.memory = {}
        self.world_model = {}

    def update(self, key, value):
        setattr(self, key, value)

    def get(self, key):
        return getattr(self, key)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2. Deterministic Agent Execution
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Make agent outputs predictable and testable.

class DeterministicAgent:

    def run(self, input_data, seed=None):

        return {
            "input": input_data,
            "result": hash(str(input_data)) % 1000
        }

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3. Task Queue System (Real Orchestration)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Replace “loops” with proper scheduling.

import asyncio

class TaskQueue:

    def __init__(self):
        self.queue = asyncio.Queue()

    async def add_task(self, task):
        await self.queue.put(task)

    async def get_task(self):
        return await self.queue.get()

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  5. Failure Recovery System (Very Important)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

This is what makes it “real system-level AI”.

class RecoverySystem:

    def recover(self, error, context):

        if "memory" in error:
            return "reload_memory_layer"

        if "tool" in error:
            return "retry_with_sandbox"

        return "escalate_to_safety_agent"

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  6. Latency Optimization Layer
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

This is where real performance comes from.

class LatencyOptimizer:

    def batch_requests(self, requests):

        return {
            "batched": True,
            "size": len(requests)
        }

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  7. Intelligent Caching System
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Prevents repeated reasoning.

class CognitiveCache:

    def __init__(self):
        self.cache = {}

    def get(self, key):
        return self.cache.get(key)

    def set(self, key, value):
        self.cache[key] = value

class HealthMonitor:

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  10. Intelligent Load Balancer
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Distributes cognitive load.

class LoadBalancer:

    def assign(self, tasks, nodes):

        assignments = {}

        for i, task in enumerate(tasks):
            node = nodes[i % len(nodes)]
            assignments[task] = node

        return assignments

Critical Design Rule

Every subsystem must answer:

What knowledge changes?
What prediction changes?
What goal changes?
What belief changes?

If it cannot answer those:
it is probably cosmetic orchestration.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Step 1: Build runtime.py first
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Everything should execute through one loop.

class CognitiveRuntime:

    def step(self,input_signal):

        self.observe(input_signal)

        self.update_world()

        self.revise_beliefs()

        self.update_goals()

        self.generate_predictions()

        action=self.plan()

        self.execute(action)

        self.reflect()

        return action

This becomes:

the cognitive heartbeat

Without this, the architecture remains disconnected modules.
